internal sealed class AggregatePartEntityConfig : IEntityTypeConfiguration<AggregatePart>
    public void Configure(EntityTypeBuilder<AggregatePart> builder)
        builder.ToTable("AggregateParts");
        builder.HasKey(e => e.PersistenceId);
        builder.Property(e => e.PersistenceId).ValueGeneratedNever();
        builder.Property(e => e.Description).UsePropertyAccessMode(PropertyAccessMode.Field).IsRequired();
        builder.HasOne(e => e.Parent) .WithMany() .HasForeignKey(e => e.ParentId) .IsRequired() .OnDelete(Cascade);

        builder.HasKey(e => e.PersistenceId);
        builder.Property(e => e.PersistenceId).ValueGeneratedNever();
        builder.ComplexProperty(e => e.Price);
